   191	    pieceId,
   192	    matchId: state.matchId,
   193	    expectedRevision: state.revision,
   194	    actor: state.currentPlayer,
   195	  };
   196	}
Tool:      1	// 随机源不接收棋盘或玩家类型；MOVE与AI均不消耗它。
     2	export const ALGORITHM = "mulberry32-v1";
     3	export const uint32 = (n) => Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 0 && n <= 0xffffffff;
     4	export function createRng(seed) {
     5	  if (!uint32(seed)) throw new Error("种子必须是uint32");
     6	  return {
     7	    algorithm: ALGORITHM,
     8	    initialSeed: seed,
     9	    state: seed,
    10	    rawDrawCount: 0,
    11	  };
    12	}
    13	export function raw(rng) {
    14	  const state = (rng.state + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
    15	  let t = Math.imul(state ^ (state >>> 15), state | 1);
    16	  t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    17	  return {
    18	    value: (t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0,
    19	    rng: { ...rng, state, rawDrawCount: rng.rawDrawCount + 1 },
    20	  };
    21	}
    22	export function bounded(rng, bound, draw = raw) {
    23	  if (!Number.isInteger(bound) || bound < 1 || bound > 0x100000000)
    24	    throw new Error("随机界限错误");
    25	  const ceiling = Math.floor(0x100000000 / bound) * bound;
    26	  let next;
    27	  do {
    28	    next = draw(rng);
    29	    rng = next.rng;
    30	  } while (next.value >= ceiling);
    31	  return { value: next.value % bound, rng };
    32	}
    33	export function freshSeed() {
    34	  return crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1))[0];
    35	}
    36	export function seedFromSearch(search) {
    37	  const value = new URLSearchParams(search).get("seed");
    38	  if (value === null) return { seed: null, error: null };
    39	  if (!/^\d+$/.test(value) || !uint32(Number(value)))
    40	    return { seed: null, error: "种子参数无效，使用公平随机新局" };
    41	  return { seed: Number(value), error: null };
    42	}
Tool:      1	import { legalMoves } from "./rules.js";
     2	// 词典序固定策略；只读合法动作，不窥视未来骰子。
     3	export function chooseMove(state, moves = legalMoves(state)) {
     4	  const score = (m) => [
     5	    Number(m.to === 56),
     6	    m.capturedIds.length,
     7	    Number(m.from === -1),
     8	    Number(m.safe),
     9	    m.to,
    10	    -Number(m.pieceId.split(":")[1]),
    11	  ];
    12	  return (
    13	    moves.slice().sort((a, b) => {
    14	      const x = score(a),
    15	        y = score(b);
    16	      for (let i = 0; i < x.length; i++) if (x[i] !== y[i]) return y[i] - x[i];
    17	      return 0;
    18	    })[0] ?? null
    19	  );
    20	}
Tool:      1	// 取消异步任务之外仍验证令牌，防止已进入队列的旧AI回调污染新局。
     2	export class TaskGate {
     3	  constructor(clock = globalThis) {
     4	    this.clock = clock;
     5	    this.token = 0;
     6	    this.timer = null;
     7	  }
     8	  cancel() {
     9	    this.token++;
    10	    if (this.timer !== null) this.clock.clearTimeout(this.timer);
    11	    this.timer = null;
    12	  }
    13	  schedule(state, delay, isCurrent, callback) {
    14	    this.cancel();
    15	    const token = this.token;
    16	    const { matchId, revision, phase } = state;
    17	    this.timer = this.clock.setTimeout(() => {
    18	      this.timer = null;
    19	      if (token === this.token && isCurrent({ matchId, revision, phase }))
    20	        callback();
    21	    }, delay);
    22	  }
    23	}
Tool:      1	import fs from 'node:fs';
     2	import path from 'node:path';
     3	import crypto from 'node:crypto';
     4	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';

     6	// 仅美术资产对账与Canvas配置序列化，不构建游戏、不执行参考源码、不处理图片。
     7	const load = p => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'));
     8	const hash = p => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(fs.readFileSync(p)).digest('hex');
     9	const spec = load('design/art-resource-spec.json');
    10	const index = load('reference-assets/research-index.json');
    11	const dice = load('reference-assets/research-dice-frames.json');
    12	const runtime = load('reference-assets/research-board-runtime.json');
    13	const root = 'public/assets/';
    14	const reuse = load('design/reuse-baseline.json');
    15	for (const item of reuse.files) assert.equal(hash(item.path), item.sha256, `上游核心已改变${item.path}`);
    16	const upstream = [...reuse.files.map(f => f.path), 'workflow/prototype.md', 'design/visual-baseline.md', 'design/acceptance.md', 'design/reuse-baseline.json'].map(p => ({path:p,sha256:hash(p)}));
    17	const assets = [];
    18	function add(file, usage, source, classification, geometry = {}) {
    19	  const p = root + file;
    20	  const bytes = fs.readFileSync(p);
    21	  const entry = {path:p, runtime_path:'assets/' + file, sha256:hash(p), bytes:bytes.length, usage, source, classification, input_format:file.endsWith('.png') ? 'WEBP' : 'MP3', ...geometry};
    22	  if (file.endsWith('.png')) {
    23	    assert.equal(bytes.subarray(0,8).toString('hex'), '89504e470d0a1a0a', `${file}不是PNG编码`);
    24	    entry.output_format = 'PNG';
    25	    entry.size = [bytes.readUInt32BE(16), bytes.readUInt32BE(20)];
    26	    entry.png_color_type = bytes[25];
    27	    assert.equal(entry.png_color_type, 6, `${file}未保留RGBA`);
    28	    if (geometry.expectedSize) assert.deepEqual(entry.size, geometry.expectedSize, `${file}尺寸不一致`);
    29	    delete entry.expectedSize;
    30	  } else entry.output_format = 'MP3';

Tool:      1	<!doctype html>
     2	<html lang="zh-CN">
     3	  <head>
     4	    <meta charset="utf-8" />
     5	    <meta
     6	      name="viewport"
     7	      content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover"
     8	    />
     9	    <meta
    10	      name="description"
    11	      content="LUDO经典飞行棋：原版十字棋盘与亮面棋子，真实摇骰、逐格跳动和吃子动画。支持2至4人同屏与人机、经典和快速对局。"
    12	    />
    13	    <title>LUDO · 原版贴图与动画版</title>
    14	    <link rel="icon" href="data:," />
    15	    <link rel="stylesheet" href="./style.css" />
    16	  </head>
    17	  <body>
    18	    <main id="scene" aria-label="LUDO游戏">
    19	      <canvas id="game" aria-label="四色十字棋盘"></canvas>
    20	      <div id="controls"></div>
    21	      <div id="menu" class="panel">
    22	        <h1>LUDO</h1>
    23	        <p>掷出六点出营 · 全部归家获胜</p>
    24	        <label
    25	          >模式
    26	          <select id="mode">
    27	            <option value="classic">经典 · 四枚归家</option>
    28	            <option value="quick">快速 · 一枚归家</option>
    29	          </select></label
    30	        >
    31	        <label
    32	          >人数
    33	          <select id="count">
    34	            <option>2</option>
    35	            <option>3</option>
    36	            <option>4</option>
    37	          </select></label
    38	        >
    39	        <label
    40	          >对局
    41	          <select id="players">
    42	            <option value="ai">人机</option>
    43	            <option value="human">同屏</option>
    44	          </select></label
    45	        >
    46	        <button id="start" class="texture" disabled>开始游戏</button>
    47	        <button id="resume" class="texture" hidden disabled>继续对局</button>
    48	        <button id="retry" class="texture" hidden>刷新重试</button>
    49	      </div>
    50	      <div id="settings" class="panel" hidden>
    51	        <h2>设置 · 已暂停</h2>
    52	        <label><input id="mute" type="checkbox" /> 静音</label>
    53	        <label><input id="reduced" type="checkbox" /> 减少动态</label>
    54	        <button id="close" class="texture">返回游戏</button>
    55	        <button id="restart" class="texture">重新选局</button>
    56	      </div>
    57	      <p id="status" aria-live="polite"></p>
    58	      <p id="error" role="alert"></p>
    59	    </main>
    60	    <script type="module" src="./src/main.js"></script>
    61	  </body>
    62	</html>
